TRIGGER when: user says /adoption-plan, "adoption plan", "customer onboarding plan", "implementation plan", "time to value", "onboarding playbook", "enablement plan", or "go-live plan". ([ashutoshsrivastava17/skill-library](https://github.com/ashutoshsrivastava17/skill-library/tree/HEAD/customer-success/skills/adoption-plan) · ★9)
- **adoption-plan**: Use when the user says "low adoption", "they are not using it", "usage has stalled", "shelfware", "seats sitting idle", "adoption plan". Produces a diagnosis of why usage is low (evidence tagged said, seen or inferred), the one cohort to move first, a 30 day plan with a pass or fail exit test, and the fallback if the cause is product fit. Not for a new customer still onboarding (use onboarding-plan), the renewal call (use renewal-risk) or an expansion pitch (use expansion-case). ([CSPulse/customer-success-skills](https://github.com/CSPulse/customer-success-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adoption-plan) · ★2)
